487→\\n 488→class HostAdapter(ABC):\\n 489→    \\\"\\\"\\\"三宿主共享的薄适配器基类。\\n 490→\\n 491→    语义面（最小输入解析、能力选择、规范状态、中断/恢复映射、产物定位）\\n 492→    全部由本类基于公共设施组装，子类不得覆写，保证三宿主语义一致；子类\\n 493→    只提供运行身份（可执行文件、安装入口、会话通道）。\\n 494→\\n 495→    越权在类定义与实例化时失败关闭（``__init_subclass__`` / ``__init__``）：\\n 496→    适配器新增公共成员、覆写共享语义面、覆写构造函数或声明\\n 497→    ``codex/hermes/omp`` 之外的宿主名都会立即报 ``HostBoundaryError``。\\n 498→    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 499→\\n 500→    host: ClassVar[HostName]\\n 501→    host_label_zh: ClassVar[str]\\n 502→\\n 503→    def __init_subclass__(cls, **kwargs: object) ->    project_root: Path,\\n 356→    host: str,\\n 357→    returncode: int,\\n 358→) -> HostRunEvidence:\\n 359→    \\\"\\\"\\\"由最终 manifest 组装运行终态证据（no-draft 断言与产物绑定）。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 360→    outcome = ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 414→\\n 415→\\n 416→def run_host_smoke(\\n 417→    host: str,\\n 418→    *,\\n 419→    project_root: Path,\\n 420→    entry: RealEntry | None = It's a self-hash — anyone with the JSON can recompute the digest after tampering. The digest protects against *accidental* drift, not malicious forgery: a forger can recompute `receipt_digest` and the model validates. The verification relies on cross-checking against \\\"project current state\\\" (manifest sha256, event stream digest, package digest, entry sha256). For a *real* forgery scenario, the forger would need to run the actual fixture (which writes the manifest + events to the project). Since manifest/events are written by the real run, a forged receipt must match current project state. The batch requires distinct project_roots per host (in the test). But the batch API takes `project_roots` dict — the caller provides roots. The cross-host forgery check only compares session_id, process.pid, run_id. If a forger runs 3 real smokes (3 distinct roots), then swaps host fields in each receipt (host=\\\"codex\\\" → \\\"hermes\\\") and recomputes digest, each single receipt would verify (as long as fixture binding and package match — they do since same package), EXCEPT: `host_executable` evidence must have name == host. If only codex is on PATH, the other two get unavailable → honest host_unavailable. In Task 9.5, presumably all three real hosts exist. A forger could run smoke with codex executable for all three, but then host_executable.path.name == \\\"codex\\\" != \\\"hermes\\\" → R05 rejects at single-receipt level. So cross-host identity swap is blocked. Good.\\n   - What about the `semantic_receipt_digest`? \\\"\\\"\\\"重新打开校验运行清单：路径、SHA-256、字节、精确 mtime_ns 与事件绑定。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 942→    path = project_root / MANIFEST_RELATIVE_PATH\\n 943→    if not path.is_file():\\n 944→        raise Not a security boundary issue since the parent runs the real smoke.\\n\\nAnother: `semantic_receipt_digest` — since the validator never recomputes it, a forged receipt could carry a wrong/arbitrary semantic digest and still pass. Impact: the semantic digest field is the link between the *host receipt* and the *adapter semantic state*; the claim in design.md that receipt binds \\\"语义回执\\\" is only structural (a 64-hex string). Since no-draft/state/artifacts are cross-checked via manifest and disk, the semantic digest is largely redundant — but the prd acceptance 6 says the receipt binds \\\"...事件链和最终 manifest\\\" — it does. The semantic digest is a soft claim. Flag as a low-severity note: validator should at least recompute semantic receipt digest deterministically or document why not (it's documented). Not a blocker.\\n\\nNow — **the biggest actual issues I found**:\\n\\n1. **`run_host_smoke` rendered-path outcome comparison bug** (host_smoke.py:501): `manifest.get(\\\"outcome\\\") != expected_outcome` compares manifest outcome (\\\"completed\\\") against catalog expected (\\\"rendered\\\") → any rendered smoke fails. Currently no rendered host-smoke case exists so tests pass; but the code intends to support rendered (exit 0 mapping, _run_evidence handles \\\"completed\\\"). Either fix (map \\\"completed\\\"→\\\"rendered\\\" for comparison) or remove the dead code. Also means Task 9.4/9.5 real-entry contract can't ever verify a successful render path through the receipt — a genuine gap for the \\\"渲染成功\\\" receipt path. Impact: Task 9.5's fresh-install verification of a successful HTML render via host receipts would fail at this comparison. If Task 9.5 only re-runs host-smoke-v1 (evidence blocked), it's masked.\\n\\n2. **Dunder-method boundary bypass** (base.py:508): `if name.startswith(\\\"_\\\"): continue` allows `__setattr__`/`__getattribute__`/`__delattr__` overrides in adapters, which can intercept `self.probe` assignment and corrupt capability gating (capability preflight results → semantic receipt), violating acceptance 3 \\\"宿主专有字段不得影响...门槛\\\". Concrete fix: explicitly reject `__setattr__`/`__getattribute__`/`__getattr__`/`__delattr__`/`__reduce__*` etc., or better, use a metaclass that freezes the class dict. Severity: medium (structural contract gap, no current adapter exploits it).\\n\\n3. **Fresh-install (Task 9.5) path resolution unverified**: `_ROOT = parents[3]` for catalog, package-manifest.json at repo root, fixtures not in package-manifest components. In a wheel install, `_installed_package_identity` and `_load_smoke_case` would fail (no package-manifest.json at site-packages root, no fixtures). Whether Task 9.5's \\\"候选包\\\" is installed as a repo copy (then `_ROOT` works) is undefined. The contract test `test_root_and_packaged_schema_are_byte_identical` covers schemas but not fixtures/manifest. Flag as a decision point for Codex: define the candidate package install layout (e.g., `uv tool install` from repo path, or wheel with package data incl. package-manifest.json + fixtures, or `--catalog` pointing to repo). This is exactly the \\\"源码级验证与 Task 9.5 边界\\\" question.\\n\\n4. **Rendered/complete receipt path is unit-tested only via `_build_receipt` with synthetic bindings**, not via `run_host_smoke` on a rendered case (because of bug #1). The artifact-binding parse logic in `_run_evidence` (host_smoke.py:382-397) is fragile string parsing of manifest outputs; combined with #1, the entire rendered host-smoke path is unexercised. Also `state == \\\"complete\\\"` requires `outcome == \\\"rendered\\\"`, but for partially_delivered state the outcome is None — `_run_evidence` only handles evidence_blocked and completed; partially_delivered manifests would raise \\\"最终 manifest 结果不支持回执绑定\\\". Fine for now (smoke case never produces partial).\\n\\n5. **`verify_host_smoke_receipt` R05 doesn't re-verify version or that the executable is the real host** — only name binding + file existence; version string from receipt. Not a gap per se (the version probe is the evidence), fine.\\n\\n6. **Host-run for host_unavailable**: the batch treats \\\"host_unavailable\\\" as not-verified (correct, honest). But note `run_host_smoke` STILL runs the fixture smoke and produces a receipt when the host executable is missing — meaning a missing host executable doesn't stop the smoke. That's the intended \\\"honest unavailable\\\" semantics. But subtle: if the host is unavailable, is it appropriate to still run the real entry and write project state? Yes — the receipt records honest failure. OK.\\n\\n7. **`_manual_inbox_pending` reads download_requests.jsonl raw JSON lines without schema validation** — a corrupted record raises ValueError inside build_semantic_receipt (not fail-closed typed error but ValueError). Actually the CLI `_capability_preflight` inline mode reorders to html first, but `project create` doesn't reorder. So `project create --outputs pdf,html` would create a project that the adapter's `resolve_minimal_input` rejects. Is there a contract-level guard? Let me check create_project_contract quickly. If the contract allows outputs not starting with html, then adapter semantic receipt fails on a valid project — a defect. My main findings:\\n\\n**Finding 1 (High, latent): run_host_smoke rendered path is broken** — outcome comparison mismatch (\\\"completed\\\" in manifest vs \\\"rendered\\\" expected from catalog). The rendered/complete receipt path is only exercised through synthetic `_build_receipt` unit tests, never through the real-entry runner. Fix: compare `manifest outcome` against the catalog's expected *run* outcome mapped the same way fixture_runner maps (\\\"completed\\\" for rendered), or normalize. Also add a rendered host-smoke test (e.g., reuse `a-complete` case with a host-smoke variant or parameterize).\\n\\nActually wait — let me double check: is it possible the catalog's expected outcome is \\\"rendered\\\" and fixture runner maps to \\\"completed\\\", and run_host_smoke expects the *manifest outcome* to equal *catalog expected outcome*? Yes: host_smoke.py line ~501: `if manifest.get(\\\"outcome\\\") != expected_outcome`. manifest outcome for rendered = \\\"completed\\\". So run_host_smoke would raise \\\"真实入口结果与预期不符\\\". Also `expected_exit` uses `_EXPECTED_EXIT_CODES[expected_outcome]` — \\\"rendered\\\"→0 — that part is consistent with the catalog vocabulary. So the bug is isolated to the manifest comparison and the exit code check after it. The receipt outcome field uses \\\"rendered\\\" for complete — `_run_evidence` maps \\\"completed\\\"→outcome \\\"rendered\\\". So the receipt layer is fine; only run_host_smoke's comparison breaks.\\n\\nLet me double-check by re-reading run_host_smoke lines 439-506: \\n- `expected_outcome = case[\\\"expected\\\"][\\\"outcome\\\"]` — \\\"evidence_blocked\\\" for host-smoke-v1.\\n- `expected_exit = _EXPECTED_EXIT_CODES.get(expected_outcome)` — 4.\\n- manifest outcome check: `manifest.get(\\\"outcome\\\") != expected_outcome`. For evidence_blocked: manifest outcome = \\\"evidence_blocked\\\" — match. For rendered: manifest outcome = \\\"completed\\\" != \\\"rendered\\\" → error. Confirmed.\\n\\n**Finding 2 (Medium): dunder-method boundary bypass** — `name.startswith(\\\"_\\\")` skips ALL non-public members including `__setattr__`, `__getattribute__`, `__getattr__`, `__delattr__`. An adapter could override `__setattr__` to intercept `self.probe = probe` in base `__init__` (instance attribute assignment goes through the class's `__setattr__`), swapping in a probe that lies about capabilities — directly violating acceptance 3 (host-specific field influencing thresholds/readiness) and the \\\"基类只允许...\\\" structural boundary. Concrete remediation: deny-list attribute-interception dunders in `__init_subclass__` or use a metaclass that freezes subclass namespace (e.g., disallow all dunders except documented ones; simplest: raise if any of `__setattr__,__getattribute__,__getattr__,__delattr__` present in `cls.__dict__`).\\n\\nNote: a private method `_swap_probe` could be called from... no public method calls it, so a private helper alone can't run. The dunder interception is the actual vector. Also `__getattribute__` override could redirect `self.probe` reads. Both are structural bypasses of the \\\"public surface frozen\\\" design intent. The test suite celebrates private helpers (test_private_host_helpers_are_still_allowed) — private data members are fine; private *behavioral overrides* are the risk.\\n\\n**Finding 3 (Medium, Task 9.5 boundary): fresh-install resource resolution is unverified and likely broken for a wheel install** — `_ROOT = parents[3]` (site-packages in a wheel) doesn't contain `fixtures/catalog.yaml` or `package-manifest.json`; `_PACKAGE_MANIFEST_CANDIDATES` won't find package-manifest.json inside site-packages; fixture catalog is not listed in package-manifest components. If Task 9.5 installs a wheel, both `run_host_smoke` (default catalog) and `_installed_package_identity` fail. If the \\\"候选包\\\" is a repo-directory copy (editable/path install), it works. Decision needed from Codex: define the install layout and add a contract test that verifies the wheel contains fixtures + package-manifest.json (or that `--catalog`/manifest path resolution works from the installed location). This is exactly the \\\"源码级验证与 Task 9.5 最终安装验收的边界\\\" question the conference is asked to challenge.\\n\\n**Finding 4 (Low-Medium): `semantic_receipt_digest` is self-asserted and never verified** — the validator keeps only structural validation (documented). A receipt can carry any 64-hex digest. This weakens the \\\"回执绑定语义回执\\\" claim in design; impact is limited because state/no-draft/artifacts are cross-checked via manifest+disk. Recommendation: either (a) make the semantic digest recomputable at verify time using the same adapter+probe path and compare, or (b) explicitly downgrade it in the design doc to \\\"运行点证据\\\" and remove it from the receipt's binding claims. Because `HostAdapter.__init__` assigns `self.probe = probe`, and instance-attribute assignment is routed through the *subclass's* `__setattr__`, a malicious/errant future adapter can:\\n```python\\ndef __setattr__(self, name, value):\\n    if name == \\\"probe\\\":\\n        value = AlwaysReadyProbe()      # or silently skip assignment\\n    super().__setattr__(name, value)\\n```\\nwhich corrupts capability gating → capability-blocked states, user messages, and (via `build_semantic_receipt`) the semantic receipt and the `semantic_receipt_digest` bound into host receipts — directly violating acceptance 3 and the \\\"基类只允许…\\\" structural contract that `test_private_host_helpers_are_still_allowed` celebrates. The test suite's freeze check (`dir(HostAdapter)`/`vars(cls)`) does not cover dunders.\\n*Remediation*: in `__init_subclass__`, explicitly reject attribute/behavior interception dunders (`__setattr__`, `__getattribute__`, `__getattr__`, `__delattr__`, `__reduce__*`, `__getstate__/__setstate__`) — or adopt a metaclass that freezes the subclass namespace. Private *data* members (the current allowed case) can remain allowed.\\n\\n**Defect C (medium, Task 9.5 boundary) — fresh-install resource resolution is undefined and wheel-install likely broken.** In a wheel install, `host_smoke.py:_ROOT = parents[3]` resolves to site-packages, where neither `fixtures/catalog.yaml` (default catalog, host_smoke.py:74) nor `package-manifest.json` (host_smoke.py:80-83) exist — `_installed_package_identity` would fail closed (\\\"安装包内找不到 package-manifest.json\\\"), and the smoke default catalog would fail. `fixtures/` is not listed in `package-manifest.json` components, and `package-manifest.json` itself is only at repo root, not inside the `ci_workflow` package dir. So the \\\"Task 9.5 fresh-install 复验用同一合同\\\" promise (PRD 8, design.md「真实入口边界」) only holds if the candidate package is installed as a repo-root copy (path/editable install), which is never stated.\\n*Remediation / decision needed*: Codex must define the candidate-package install layout for Task 9.5 (wheel-with-bundled-fixtures-and-manifest vs. repo-directory install), and a contract test must assert the packaged layout resolves `fixtures/catalog.yaml` + `package-manifest.json` from the installed location. This is the exact \\\"源码级验证与 Task 9.5 最终安装验收的边界\\\" the conference objective asks to challenge.\\n\\n**Defect D (low-medium) — `semantic_receipt_digest` is self-asserted, unverifiable, and decoration-only in the verification path.** `host_smoke.py:603` documents that the validator never recomputes it (\\\"运行时点稳定\\\"); therefore any 64-hex string passes R01–R09. The design.md claim that the receipt \\\"绑定…语义回执\\\" is structurally weaker than it reads. Impact is bounded (state/no-draft/artifacts are cross-checked against manifest + disk), but the field adds forgery surface without verification.\\n*Remediation options*: (a) downgrade the claim in design.md to \\\"运行点证据，不参与验证\\\"; or (b) make verification recompute it deterministically where possible (it is deterministic *given* a probe snapshot — the flakiness is the runtime probe; a StaticCapabilityProbe-based recompute would only work in tests).\\n\\n**Defect E (low) — `_manual_inbox_pending` hard-aborts on any malformed record** (`base.py:306-307` raises bare `ValueError`), which fails *all three* hosts' semantic receipts on one corrupt/legacy jsonl line, and the error is not a typed contract error.
